# Third-party notices

The hikyaku binaries link the Go modules below. Each license text appears once,
with the modules it covers, followed by the NOTICE files of the modules that
ship one. `make notices` regenerates this file and THIRD_PARTY_LICENSES.csv.

## Licenses

with a list of developers who could not be reached.
